{"forms":[{"form":"gets through","tags":["present","singular","third-person"]},{"form":"getting through","tags":["participle","present"]},{"form":"got through","tags":["past"]},{"form":"got through","tags":["UK","participle","past"]},{"form":"gotten through","tags":["US","participle","past"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"verb","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"Military training was hard, but I got through it.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To overcome; to endure."],"tags":["informal","transitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"She got through her book this morning!","type":"example"},{"text":"I was so thirsty that I got through three glasses of orange juice.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To complete; to finish."],"tags":["informal","transitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"With near-daily trips to Houston and back, he really gets through a lot of fuel in one month.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To use up; to expend."],"synonyms":[{"word":"burn through"},{"word":"run through"}],"tags":["informal","transitive"]},{"glosses":["To reach its destination."],"tags":["informal","intransitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"He was such a dyed-in-the-wool partisan that I just couldn't get through to him and change his mind.","type":"example"},{"text":"He was such a dyed-in-the-wool partisan that I just couldn't get it through his skull.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To communicate successfully across a barrier; to make contact, or get one's point across; to reach the addressee, possibly with difficulty."],"synonyms":[{"word":"patch"},{"word":"get across"}],"tags":["informal","intransitive","transitive"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/ɡɛt ˈθɹu/"}],"upstream_line":1223441,"upstream_offset":4337707354,"upstream_record_sha256":"0101d914ee3826dd39ffc7cf91d2241778c9ed98f2a431d526a91e8fa8905192","word":"get through"}
